# Summer 2027 Tech Internships & New Grad Jobs (USA)

Open software, data and AI internships at US companies for students graduating in 2027 and 2028, plus new grad and entry-level roles. The list refreshes every 4 hours: closed postings drop off and new ones appear at the top.

- **[Software & Data Internships](README.md)**: **1,280** open ([FAANG+](README.md#faang) (366) · [Quant & Trading](README.md#quant--trading) (37) · [Other](README.md#other) (877))
- **[New Grad & Entry-Level Jobs](NEW_GRAD.md)**: **490** open ([FAANG+](NEW_GRAD.md#faang) (160) · [Quant & Trading](NEW_GRAD.md#quant--trading) (25) · [Other](NEW_GRAD.md#other) (305))
- **[Hardware & Engineering Internships](ENGINEERING_INTERNSHIPS.md)**: **1,410** open ([FAANG+](ENGINEERING_INTERNSHIPS.md#faang) (82) · [Quant & Trading](ENGINEERING_INTERNSHIPS.md#quant--trading) (5) · [Other](ENGINEERING_INTERNSHIPS.md#other) (1323))
- **[Business, Finance & Other Internships](BUSINESS_INTERNSHIPS.md)**: **1,694** open ([FAANG+](BUSINESS_INTERNSHIPS.md#faang) (120) · [Quant & Trading](BUSINESS_INTERNSHIPS.md#quant--trading) (19) · [Other](BUSINESS_INTERNSHIPS.md#other) (1555))

<!-- SUMMARY_END -->

Search and filter every role on **[TalentdPro](https://www.talentdpro.com/us/entry-level-jobs?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme&utm_campaign=us-internships)**.

**How to use it:** roles are sorted newest first, so the top of each table is what opened most recently. Apply links go straight to the employer's own posting. Internship pay is shown per hour and full-time pay per year, converted from what the employer listed; it's blank when they didn't say or the figure doesn't look right.

Know of a role that's missing, or one that has closed? [Add a role](../../issues/new?template=new_role.yml) or [report a fix](../../issues/new?template=fix_role.yml); see [CONTRIBUTING](CONTRIBUTING.md).
